# Weapons

Every weapon class: pistols, rifles, shotguns, machine guns, launchers, the special guns (net, leech, injection, polarity ...) and melee. 4,857 templates under 295 names. Back to the [item list](../gm-items.md).

Type the command in chat on an Admin account; the item goes into your own inventory.
